DOSYA: app\analytics\add_youtube_id.py

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE ID KOLONU
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DB_FILE = BASE_DIR / "data" / "creator_engine.db"


def add_youtube_id():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  CREATOR ENGINE â€” DB GÃœNCELLEME")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    db = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
    cursor = db.cursor()

    columns = cursor.execute(
        "PRAGMA table_info(videos)"
    ).fetchall()

    column_names = [
        column[1]
        for column in columns
    ]

    if "youtube_id" in column_names:

        print("â„¹ï¸ youtube_id kolonu zaten mevcut.")

    else:

        cursor.execute(
            """
            ALTER TABLE videos
            ADD COLUMN youtube_id TEXT
            """
        )

        db.commit()

        print(
            "âœ… youtube_id kolonu baÅŸarÄ±yla eklendi."
        )

    db.close()

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… DB GÃœNCELLEMESÄ° TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":

    add_youtube_id()

import json

from openai import OpenAI

from config.settings import OPENAI_API_KEY

from app.analytics.analytics_engine import analyze_videos


client = OpenAI(
    api_key=OPENAI_API_KEY
)


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” AI STRATEGY ANALYST
# ============================================================

MINIMUM_VIDEOS_FOR_AI = 10


def build_ai_strategy():

    # ========================================================
    # ANALYTICS VERÄ°SÄ°NÄ° AL
    # ========================================================

    analysis = analyze_videos()

    total_videos = analysis[
        "total_videos"
    ]

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  AI STRATEGY ANALYST")
    print("========================================")

    print()
    print(
        f"ğŸ“Š Analiz edilen video: "
        f"{total_videos}"
    )

    # ========================================================
    # YETERLÄ° VERÄ° YOK
    # ========================================================

    if total_videos < MINIMUM_VIDEOS_FOR_AI:

        print()
        print(
            f"ğŸŸ¡ AI analizi iÃ§in en az "
            f"{MINIMUM_VIDEOS_FOR_AI} video gerekli."
        )

        print(
            f"ğŸ“¦ Åu anda {total_videos} video var."
        )

        print(
            "ğŸ’° AI Ã§aÄŸrÄ±sÄ± yapÄ±lmadÄ±."
        )

        return {
            "status": "INSUFFICIENT_DATA",
            "total_videos": total_videos,
            "strategy": None,
        }

    # ========================================================
    # PERFORMANS KONTROLÃœ
    # ========================================================

    if not analysis[
        "performance_available"
    ]:

        print()
        print(
            "ğŸŸ¡ HenÃ¼z performans verisi yok."
        )

        print(
            "AI strateji analizi yapÄ±lmayacak."
        )

        return {
            "status": "NO_PERFORMANCE_DATA",
            "total_videos": total_videos,
            "strategy": None,
        }

    # ========================================================
    # AI'YA GÃ–NDERÄ°LECEK Ã–ZET
    # ========================================================

    analytics_summary = {

        "total_videos":
            analysis["total_videos"],

        "average_duration":
            analysis["average_duration"],

        "average_images":
            analysis["average_images"],

        "total_views":
            analysis["total_views"],

        "total_likes":
            analysis["total_likes"],

        "total_comments":
            analysis["total_comments"],

        "average_views":
            analysis["average_views"],

        "average_likes":
            analysis["average_likes"],

        "average_comments":
            analysis["average_comments"],

        "top_topics":
            analysis["top_topics"],

        "best_video":
            analysis["best_video"],
    }

    # ========================================================
    # AI Ä°STEMÄ°
    # ========================================================

    prompt = f"""
Sen bir YouTube Shorts strateji analistisin.

Sana bir kanalÄ±n performans verilerinin
Ã¶zetini veriyorum.

Bu verileri analiz et ve gelecekteki
Shorts Ã¼retimi iÃ§in uygulanabilir bir
strateji oluÅŸtur.

Ã–NEMLÄ° KURALLAR:

- Veride olmayan bilgileri uydurma.
- Kesin olmayan sonuÃ§larÄ± kesin gerÃ§ek gibi sunma.
- KÃ¼Ã§Ã¼k veri miktarlarÄ±nda dikkatli yorum yap.
- Ä°zlenme verisi varsa performansa gÃ¶re yorum yap.
- Konu performansÄ±nÄ± dikkate al.
- Video sÃ¼resini dikkate al.
- GÃ¶rsel yoÄŸunluÄŸunu dikkate al.
- Ä°zlenme, beÄŸeni ve yorum iliÅŸkisini deÄŸerlendir.
- BaÅŸarÄ±lÄ± olan Ã¶zellikleri tespit etmeye Ã§alÄ±ÅŸ.
- Gelecekte test edilmesi gereken fikirler Ã¶ner.
- AynÄ± konuyu sÃ¼rekli tekrar etmeyi Ã¶nerme.
- KanalÄ±n Ã§eÅŸitliliÄŸini koru.
- Stratejiyi doÄŸrudan Content Engine'in
  kullanabileceÄŸi ÅŸekilde yaz.

SADECE JSON DÃ–NDÃœR.

FORMAT:

{{
    "summary": "KanalÄ±n genel durumu",
    "winning_topics": [
        "baÅŸarÄ±lÄ± konu 1",
        "baÅŸarÄ±lÄ± konu 2"
    ],
    "recommended_topics": [
        "Ã¶nerilen konu 1",
        "Ã¶nerilen konu 2",
        "Ã¶nerilen konu 3"
    ],
    "duration_strategy": "Video sÃ¼re stratejisi",
    "visual_strategy": "GÃ¶rsel stratejisi",
    "hook_strategy": "Hook stratejisi",
    "content_strategy": "Genel iÃ§erik stratejisi",
    "tests": [
        "test edilmesi gereken ÅŸey 1",
        "test edilmesi gereken ÅŸey 2"
    ],
    "confidence": "LOW"
}}

KANAL ANALÄ°Z VERÄ°SÄ°:

{json.dumps(
    analytics_summary,
    ensure_ascii=False,
    indent=2
)}
"""

    # ========================================================
    # AI Ã‡AÄRISI
    # ========================================================

    print()
    print(
        "ğŸ¤– AI kanal stratejisini analiz ediyor..."
    )

    response = client.responses.create(
        model="gpt-5.4-mini",
        input=prompt,
    )

    raw = response.output_text.strip()

    # ========================================================
    # MARKDOWN TEMÄ°ZLE
    # ========================================================

    if raw.startswith("```json"):

        raw = raw[
            len("```json"):
        ].strip()

    if raw.endswith("```"):

        raw = raw[
            :-3
        ].strip()

    # ========================================================
    # JSON
    # ========================================================

    try:

        strategy = json.loads(raw)

    except json.JSONDecodeError as error:

        print()
        print(
            "âŒ AI JSON Ã§Ä±ktÄ±sÄ± okunamadÄ±."
        )

        print()
        print("AI Ã§Ä±ktÄ±sÄ±:")
        print(raw)

        raise ValueError(
            "AI strateji Ã§Ä±ktÄ±sÄ± geÃ§erli JSON deÄŸil."
        ) from error

    # ========================================================
    # SONUÃ‡
    # ========================================================

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ¯ AI STRATEJÄ°SÄ°")
    print("========================================")

    print()
    print(
        f"ğŸ“Œ Ã–zet:\n"
        f"{strategy.get('summary', '')}"
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ† BAÅARILI KONULAR")

    for topic in strategy.get(
        "winning_topics",
        []
    ):

        print(
            f"â€¢ {topic}"
        )

    print()
    print("ğŸ’¡ Ã–NERÄ°LEN KONULAR")

    for topic in strategy.get(
        "recommended_topics",
        []
    ):

        print(
            f"â€¢ {topic}"
        )

    print()
    print("â±ï¸ SÃœRE STRATEJÄ°SÄ°")
    print(
        strategy.get(
            "duration_strategy",
            ""
        )
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ–¼ï¸ GÃ–RSEL STRATEJÄ°SÄ°")
    print(
        strategy.get(
            "visual_strategy",
            ""
        )
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ£ HOOK STRATEJÄ°SÄ°")
    print(
        strategy.get(
            "hook_strategy",
            ""
        )
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ¯ Ä°Ã‡ERÄ°K STRATEJÄ°SÄ°")
    print(
        strategy.get(
            "content_strategy",
            ""
        )
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ§ª TESTLER")

    for test in strategy.get(
        "tests",
        []
    ):

        print(
            f"â€¢ {test}"
        )

    print()
    print(
        "ğŸ“ˆ GÃ¼ven seviyesi: "
        f"{strategy.get('confidence', 'LOW')}"
    )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… AI STRATEJÄ° ANALÄ°ZÄ° TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")

    return {
        "status": "OK",
        "total_videos": total_videos,
        "strategy": strategy,
    }


# ============================================================
# TEST
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    build_ai_strategy()

============================================================
DOSYA: app\analytics\analytics_engine.py
============================================================
from collections import Counter

from app.analytics.database import (
    get_all_videos,
    initialize_database,
)


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” ANALYTICS ENGINE 2.0
# ============================================================

def analyze_videos():

    initialize_database()

    videos = get_all_videos()

    # ========================================================
    # VERÄ° YOK
    # ========================================================

    if not videos:

        return {
            "total_videos": 0,
            "average_duration": 0,
            "average_images": 0,
            "total_views": 0,
            "total_likes": 0,
            "total_comments": 0,
            "average_views": 0,
            "average_likes": 0,
            "average_comments": 0,
            "top_topics": [],
            "best_video": None,
            "top_videos": [],
            "worst_videos": [],
            "duration_performance": [],
            "engagement_rate": 0,
            "average_watch_time": 0,
            "average_watch_percentage": 0,
            "above_average_videos": 0,
            "performance_available": False,
        }

    # ========================================================
    # TEMEL VERÄ°LER
    # ========================================================

    total_videos = len(videos)

    durations = [
        video["duration"]
        for video in videos
        if video["duration"] is not None
        and video["duration"] > 0
    ]

    image_counts = [
        video["image_count"]
        for video in videos
        if video["image_count"] is not None
        and video["image_count"] > 0
    ]

    total_views = sum(
        video["views"] or 0
        for video in videos
    )

    total_likes = sum(
        video["likes"] or 0
        for video in videos
    )

    total_comments = sum(
        video["comments"] or 0
        for video in videos
    )

    # ========================================================
    # ORTALAMALAR
    # ========================================================

    average_duration = (
        sum(durations) / len(durations)
        if durations
        else 0
    )

    average_images = (
        sum(image_counts) / len(image_counts)
        if image_counts
        else 0
    )

    average_views = (
        total_views / total_videos
        if total_videos
        else 0
    )

    average_likes = (
        total_likes / total_videos
        if total_videos
        else 0
    )

    average_comments = (
        total_comments / total_videos
        if total_videos
        else 0
    )

    # ========================================================
    # PERFORMANS VERÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    performance_videos = [
        video
        for video in videos
        if (video["views"] or 0) > 0
    ]

    performance_available = bool(
        performance_videos
    )

    # ========================================================
    # WATCH TIME
    # ========================================================

    watch_time_values = [
        video["average_watch_time"]
        for video in videos
        if video["average_watch_time"] is not None
        and video["average_watch_time"] > 0
    ]

    watch_percentage_values = [
        video["watch_percentage"]
        for video in videos
        if video["watch_percentage"] is not None
        and video["watch_percentage"] > 0
    ]

    average_watch_time = (
        sum(watch_time_values)
        / len(watch_time_values)
        if watch_time_values
        else 0
    )

    average_watch_percentage = (
        sum(watch_percentage_values)
        / len(watch_percentage_values)
        if watch_percentage_values
        else 0
    )

    # ========================================================
    # ETKÄ°LEÅÄ°M ORANI
    # ========================================================

    engagement_rate = (
        (
            total_likes
            + total_comments
        )
        / total_views
        * 100
        if total_views > 0
        else 0
    )

    # ========================================================
    # KONULAR
    # ========================================================

    topics = [
        video["topic"]
        for video in videos
        if video["topic"]
    ]

    topic_counter = Counter(topics)

    top_topics = [
        {
            "topic": topic,
            "count": count,
        }
        for topic, count
        in topic_counter.most_common(10)
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # EN Ä°YÄ° VÄ°DEOLAR
    # ========================================================

    sorted_videos = sorted(
        performance_videos,
        key=lambda video: (
            video["views"] or 0
        ),
        reverse=True,
    )

    top_videos = [
        {
            "video_number":
                video["video_number"],

            "topic":
                video["topic"],

            "views":
                video["views"] or 0,

            "likes":
                video["likes"] or 0,

            "comments":
                video["comments"] or 0,

            "duration":
                video["duration"] or 0,

            "watch_percentage":
                video["watch_percentage"] or 0,
        }
        for video in sorted_videos[:10]
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # EN KÃ–TÃœ VÄ°DEOLAR
    # ========================================================

    worst_sorted = sorted(
        performance_videos,
        key=lambda video: (
            video["views"] or 0
        ),
    )

    worst_videos = [
        {
            "video_number":
                video["video_number"],

            "topic":
                video["topic"],

            "views":
                video["views"] or 0,

            "duration":
                video["duration"] or 0,
        }
        for video in worst_sorted[:10]
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # EN BAÅARILI VÄ°DEO
    # ========================================================

    best_video = None

    if sorted_videos:

        best = sorted_videos[0]

        best_video = {
            "video_number":
                best["video_number"],

            "topic":
                best["topic"],

            "views":
                best["views"] or 0,

            "likes":
                best["likes"] or 0,

            "comments":
                best["comments"] or 0,

            "duration":
                best["duration"] or 0,

            "watch_percentage":
                best["watch_percentage"] or 0,
        }

    # ========================================================
    # ORTALAMA ÃœZERÄ° VÄ°DEOLAR
    # ========================================================

    above_average_videos = len([
        video
        for video in performance_videos
        if (video["views"] or 0)
        > average_views
    ])

    # ========================================================
    # SÃœRE PERFORMANSI
    # ========================================================

    duration_groups = {
        "0-20 sn": [],
        "21-30 sn": [],
        "31-45 sn": [],
        "46-60 sn": [],
        "61+ sn": [],
    }

    for video in performance_videos:

        duration = video["duration"] or 0
        views = video["views"] or 0

        if duration <= 20:

            duration_groups[
                "0-20 sn"
            ].append(views)

        elif duration <= 30:

            duration_groups[
                "21-30 sn"
            ].append(views)

        elif duration <= 45:

            duration_groups[
                "31-45 sn"
            ].append(views)

        elif duration <= 60:

            duration_groups[
                "46-60 sn"
            ].append(views)

        else:

            duration_groups[
                "61+ sn"
            ].append(views)

    duration_performance = []

    for group, values in duration_groups.items():

        if values:

            duration_performance.append({
                "range": group,
                "video_count": len(values),
                "average_views": round(
                    sum(values) / len(values),
                    2
                ),
                "total_views": sum(values),
            })

    # ========================================================
    # SONUÃ‡
    # ========================================================

    return {

        "total_videos":
            total_videos,

        "average_duration":
            round(
                average_duration,
                2
            ),

        "average_images":
            round(
                average_images,
                2
            ),

        "total_views":
            total_views,

        "total_likes":
            total_likes,

        "total_comments":
            total_comments,

        "average_views":
            round(
                average_views,
                2
            ),

        "average_likes":
            round(
                average_likes,
                2
            ),

        "average_comments":
            round(
                average_comments,
                2
            ),

        "top_topics":
            top_topics,

        "best_video":
            best_video,

        "top_videos":
            top_videos,

        "worst_videos":
            worst_videos,

        "duration_performance":
            duration_performance,

        "engagement_rate":
            round(
                engagement_rate,
                2
            ),

        "average_watch_time":
            round(
                average_watch_time,
                2
            ),

        "average_watch_percentage":
            round(
                average_watch_percentage,
                2
            ),

        "above_average_videos":
            above_average_videos,

        "performance_available":
            performance_available,
    }

from app.analytics.database import (
    get_all_videos,
    initialize_database,
)


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” ANALYTICS RAPORU
# ============================================================

def show_report():

    initialize_database()

    videos = get_all_videos()

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  CREATOR ENGINE ANALYTICS")
    print("========================================")

    if not videos:

        print()
        print("HenÃ¼z Analytics verisi bulunmuyor.")
        print()

        return

    # ========================================================
    # TEMEL VERÄ°LER
    # ========================================================

    total_videos = len(videos)

    durations = [
        video["duration"]
        for video in videos
        if video["duration"] is not None
        and video["duration"] > 0
    ]

    image_counts = [
        video["image_count"]
        for video in videos
        if video["image_count"] is not None
        and video["image_count"] > 0
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # ORTALAMALAR
    # ========================================================

    average_duration = (
        sum(durations) / len(durations)
        if durations
        else 0
    )

    average_images = (
        sum(image_counts) / len(image_counts)
        if image_counts
        else 0
    )

    # ========================================================
    # EN UZUN / EN KISA
    # ========================================================

    longest_video = (
        max(
            videos,
            key=lambda video: video["duration"]
        )
        if videos
        else None
    )

    shortest_video = (
        min(
            [
                video
                for video in videos
                if video["duration"] > 0
            ],
            key=lambda video: video["duration"]
        )
        if durations
        else None
    )

    # ========================================================
    # RAPOR
    # ========================================================

    print()
    print(f"ğŸ¬ Toplam video       : {total_videos}")

    print(
        f"â±ï¸ Ortalama sÃ¼re      : "
        f"{average_duration:.2f} saniye"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ–¼ï¸ Ortalama gÃ¶rsel    : "
        f"{average_images:.2f}"
    )

    # ========================================================
    # EN UZUN
    # ========================================================

    if longest_video:

        print()
        print("â±ï¸ EN UZUN VÄ°DEO")
        print(
            f"Video: "
            f"{longest_video['video_number']}"
        )
        print(
            f"SÃ¼re: "
            f"{longest_video['duration']:.2f} saniye"
        )
        print(
            f"Konu: "
            f"{longest_video['topic']}"
        )

    # ========================================================
    # EN KISA
    # ========================================================

    if shortest_video:

        print()
        print("âš¡ EN KISA VÄ°DEO")
        print(
            f"Video: "
            f"{shortest_video['video_number']}"
        )
        print(
            f"SÃ¼re: "
            f"{shortest_video['duration']:.2f} saniye"
        )
        print(
            f"Konu: "
            f"{shortest_video['topic']}"
        )

    # ========================================================
    # VÄ°DEO LÄ°STESÄ°
    # ========================================================

    print()
    print("----------------------------------------")
    print("ğŸ“‹ VÄ°DEOLAR")
    print("----------------------------------------")

    for video in videos:

        print(
            f"#{video['video_number']:03d} | "
            f"{video['duration']:.2f} sn | "
            f"{video['image_count']} gÃ¶rsel | "
            f"{video['topic']}"
        )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… ANALYTICS RAPORU TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")


# ============================================================
# Ã‡ALIÅTIR
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    show_report()

from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” ANALYTICS DATABASE
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
DATABASE_FILE = DATA_DIR / "creator_engine.db"


# ============================================================
# VERÄ°TABANI BAÄLANTISI
# ============================================================

def get_connection():

    DATA_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True
    )

    connection = sqlite3.connect(
        DATABASE_FILE
    )

    connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

    return connection


# ============================================================
# TABLOLARI OLUÅTUR
# ============================================================

def initialize_database():

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS videos (

            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

            video_number INTEGER,

            topic TEXT NOT NULL,

            category TEXT,

            hook TEXT,

            duration REAL,

            image_count INTEGER,

            created_at TEXT,

            published_at TEXT,

            views INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            likes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            comments INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            shares INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            average_watch_time REAL DEFAULT 0,

            watch_percentage REAL DEFAULT 0

        )
        """
    )

    connection.commit()

    connection.close()

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  ANALYTICS DATABASE")
    print("========================================")
    print()
    print("âœ… VeritabanÄ± hazÄ±r.")
    print()
    print(f"ğŸ“ {DATABASE_FILE}")


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEO EKLE
# ============================================================

def add_video(
    video_number,
    topic,
    category=None,
    hook=None,
    duration=0,
    image_count=0,
    created_at=None,
):

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO videos (
            video_number,
            topic,
            category,
            hook,
            duration,
            image_count,
            created_at
        )

        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """,
        (
            video_number,
            topic,
            category,
            hook,
            duration,
            image_count,
            created_at,
        )
    )

    connection.commit()

    video_id = cursor.lastrowid

    connection.close()

    return video_id


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEO Ä°STATÄ°STÄ°KLERÄ°NÄ° GÃœNCELLE
# ============================================================

def update_video_stats(
    video_id,
    views=0,
    likes=0,
    comments=0,
    shares=0,
    average_watch_time=0,
    watch_percentage=0,
):

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        UPDATE videos

        SET
            views = ?,
            likes = ?,
            comments = ?,
            shares = ?,
            average_watch_time = ?,
            watch_percentage = ?

        WHERE id = ?
        """,
        (
            views,
            likes,
            comments,
            shares,
            average_watch_time,
            watch_percentage,
            video_id,
        )
    )

    connection.commit()

    connection.close()


# ============================================================
# TÃœM VÄ°DEOLARI GETÄ°R
# ============================================================

def get_all_videos():

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        SELECT *
        FROM videos
        ORDER BY id ASC
        """
    )

    videos = cursor.fetchall()

    connection.close()

    return videos


# ============================================================
# TEST
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    initialize_database()

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” DATABASE SCHEMA
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DB_FILE = BASE_DIR / "data" / "creator_engine.db"


def show_schema():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  CREATOR ENGINE â€” DATABASE SCHEMA")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    print(f"ğŸ“ VeritabanÄ±: {DB_FILE}")
    print()

    db = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)
    cursor = db.cursor()

    tables = cursor.execute(
        """
        SELECT name
        FROM sqlite_master
        WHERE type = 'table'
        ORDER BY name
        """
    ).fetchall()

    print("=== TABLOLAR ===")
    print()

    for table in tables:

        table_name = table[0]

        print(f"ğŸ“¦ {table_name}")

        columns = cursor.execute(
            f'PRAGMA table_info("{table_name}")'
        ).fetchall()

        for column in columns:

            print(
                f"   {column[1]} | "
                f"{column[2]} | "
                f"PK={column[5]}"
            )

        print()

    db.close()

    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… ÅEMA OKUMA TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":

    show_schema()

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import subprocess

from app.analytics.database import (
    get_connection,
    initialize_database,
)


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” ESKÄ° VÄ°DEOLARI ANALYTICS'E AKTAR
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

SHORTS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "shorts"


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEO SÃœRESÄ°NÄ° AL
# ============================================================

def get_video_duration(video_file):

    try:

        result = subprocess.run(
            [
                "ffprobe",
                "-v",
                "error",
                "-show_entries",
                "format=duration",
                "-of",
                "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1",
                str(video_file),
            ],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            check=True,
        )

        return float(
            result.stdout.strip()
        )

    except Exception as error:

        print()
        print(
            f"âš ï¸ Video sÃ¼resi alÄ±namadÄ±: {error}"
        )

        return 0.0


# ============================================================
# GÃ–RSEL SAYISINI BUL
# ============================================================

def get_image_count(image_folder):

    if not image_folder.exists():

        return 0

    return len(
        [
            file
            for file in image_folder.iterdir()
            if file.is_file()
            and file.suffix.lower()
            in [
                ".png",
                ".jpg",
                ".jpeg",
                ".webp",
            ]
        ]
    )


# ============================================================
# SENARYODAN HOOK AL
# ============================================================

def get_hook(script_file):

    if not script_file.exists():

        return ""

    try:

        lines = [
            line.strip()
            for line in script_file.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            ).splitlines()
            if line.strip()
        ]

        if lines:

            return lines[0]

    except Exception as error:

        print()
        print(
            f"âš ï¸ Senaryo okunamadÄ±: {error}"
        )

    return ""


# ============================================================
# VERÄ°TABANINDA VÄ°DEO VAR MI?
# ============================================================

def video_exists(video_number):

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        SELECT id
        FROM videos
        WHERE video_number = ?
        LIMIT 1
        """,
        (video_number,),
    )

    result = cursor.fetchone()

    connection.close()

    return result is not None

# VÄ°DEOYU AKTAR
# ============================================================

def import_video(video_number):

    video_folder = (
        SHORTS_DIR
        / f"{video_number:03d}"
    )

    video_file = (
        video_folder
        / "video.mp4"
    )

    image_folder = (
        video_folder
        / "gorseller"
    )

    script_file = (
        video_folder
        / "senaryo.txt"
    )

    if not video_file.exists():

        print(
            f"âš ï¸ #{video_number:03d} video.mp4 bulunamadÄ±."
        )

        return False

    if video_exists(video_number):

        print(
            f"â­ï¸ #{video_number:03d} zaten Analytics'te var."
        )

        return False

    print()
    print(
        "----------------------------------------"
    )
    print(
        f"ğŸ¬ VIDEO #{video_number:03d}"
    )
    print(
        "----------------------------------------"
    )

    print()

    topic = input(
        "ğŸ“Œ Konu: "
    ).strip()

    while not topic:

        print(
            "âš ï¸ Konu boÅŸ bÄ±rakÄ±lamaz."
        )

        topic = input(
            "ğŸ“Œ Konu: "
        ).strip()

    hook = get_hook(
        script_file
    )

    duration = get_video_duration(
        video_file
    )

    image_count = get_image_count(
        image_folder
    )

    created_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(
        video_file.stat().st_mtime
    ).isoformat(
        timespec="seconds"
    )

    connection = get_connection()

    cursor = connection.cursor()

    cursor.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO videos (
            video_number,
            topic,
            category,
            hook,
            duration,
            image_count,
            created_at
        )

        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """,
        (
            video_number,
            topic,
            None,
            hook,
            duration,
            image_count,
            created_at,
        ),
    )

    connection.commit()

    analytics_id = cursor.lastrowid

    connection.close()

    print()
    print(
        "âœ… Analytics'e eklendi."
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ§  ID: {analytics_id}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ“¹ Video: #{video_number:03d}"
    )

    print(
        f"â±ï¸ SÃ¼re: {duration:.2f} saniye"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ–¼ï¸ GÃ¶rsel: {image_count}"
    )

    return True

from pathlib import Path
import json

def build_strategy():

    analysis = analyze_videos()

    total_videos = analysis["total_videos"]

    # ========================================================
    # VERÄ° YOK
    # ========================================================

    if total_videos == 0:

        return {
            "status": "NO_DATA",
            "message": (
                "Strateji oluÅŸturmak iÃ§in "
                "yeterli veri yok."
            ),
        }

    # ========================================================
    # TEMEL VERÄ°LER
    # ========================================================

    average_duration = analysis[
        "average_duration"
    ]

    average_images = analysis[
        "average_images"
    ]

    top_topics = analysis[
        "top_topics"
    ]

    best_video = analysis[
        "best_video"
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # SÃœRE STRATEJÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    if average_duration <= 30:

        duration_strategy = (
            "KÄ±sa videolar tercih edilmeli. "
            "YaklaÅŸÄ±k 25-35 saniye hedeflenebilir."
        )

    elif average_duration <= 45:

        duration_strategy = (
            "Mevcut sÃ¼re aralÄ±ÄŸÄ± uygun gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼yor. "
            "YaklaÅŸÄ±k 30-45 saniye hedeflenebilir."
        )

    else:

        duration_strategy = (
            "Videolar nispeten uzun. "
            "Gereksiz bÃ¶lÃ¼mler azaltÄ±larak "
            "30-45 saniye aralÄ±ÄŸÄ± test edilebilir."
        )

    # ========================================================
    # GÃ–RSEL STRATEJÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    if average_images <= 4:

        image_strategy = (
            "Video baÅŸÄ±na yaklaÅŸÄ±k 4-5 gÃ¶rsel "
            "kullanÄ±lmasÄ± test edilebilir."
        )

    elif average_images <= 6:

        image_strategy = (
            "Mevcut gÃ¶rsel yoÄŸunluÄŸu uygun. "
            "YaklaÅŸÄ±k 5 gÃ¶rsel kullanÄ±labilir."
        )

    else:

        image_strategy = (
            "GÃ¶rsel sayÄ±sÄ± yÃ¼ksek. "
            "Sadece Ã¶nemli sahneler iÃ§in gÃ¶rsel "
            "Ã¼retilmesi maliyeti azaltabilir."
        )

    # ========================================================
    # KONU STRATEJÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    if top_topics:

        topic_strategy = (
            "Mevcut verilerde kullanÄ±lan konular: "
            + ", ".join(
                item["topic"]
                for item in top_topics[:5]
            )
        )

        winning_topics = [
            item["topic"]
            for item in top_topics[:5]
        ]

    else:

        topic_strategy = (
            "HenÃ¼z yeterli konu verisi bulunmuyor."
        )

        winning_topics = []

    # ========================================================
    # Ã–NERÄ°LEN KONULAR
    # ========================================================

    recommended_topics = [
        "Yapay zekanÄ±n gÃ¼nlÃ¼k hayattaki ÅŸaÅŸÄ±rtÄ±cÄ± etkileri",
        "Ä°nsan beyninin bilinmeyen Ã¶zellikleri",
        "Uzay ve gelecekte yaÅŸam",
        "Bilimin gÃ¼nlÃ¼k hayattaki ÅŸaÅŸÄ±rtÄ±cÄ± gerÃ§ekleri",
        "Teknolojinin gelecekte deÄŸiÅŸtireceÄŸi ÅŸeyler",
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # HOOK STRATEJÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    hook_strategy = (
        "Ä°lk cÃ¼mlede doÄŸrudan merak uyandÄ±ran "
        "bir soru, ÅŸaÅŸÄ±rtÄ±cÄ± bilgi veya gÃ¼Ã§lÃ¼ "
        "iddia kullanÄ±lmalÄ±. Gereksiz giriÅŸ "
        "yapÄ±lmamalÄ±."
    )

    # ========================================================
    # Ä°Ã‡ERÄ°K STRATEJÄ°SÄ°
    # ========================================================

    content_strategy = (
        "Ä°Ã§erikler kÄ±sa, hÄ±zlÄ± ve bilgi odaklÄ± "
        "olmalÄ±. Ä°lk saniyelerde gÃ¼Ã§lÃ¼ bir hook "
        "kullanÄ±lmalÄ±. Konu boyunca merak korunmalÄ± "
        "ve sonunda izleyiciye soru sorulmalÄ±."
    )

    # ========================================================
    # TEST Ã–NERÄ°LERÄ°
    # ========================================================

    tests = [
        "30-45 saniye aralÄ±ÄŸÄ±nÄ± koru.",
        "Video baÅŸÄ±na yaklaÅŸÄ±k 5 gÃ¶rsel kullan.",
        "FarklÄ± hook tÃ¼rlerini test et.",
        "Bilgi + merak kombinasyonunu test et.",
        "Benzer konularÄ± farklÄ± aÃ§Ä±lardan dene.",
    ]

    # ========================================================
    # EN Ä°YÄ° VÄ°DEO
    # ========================================================

    best_video_strategy = None

    if best_video:

        best_video_strategy = {

            "video_number":
                best_video["video_number"],

            "topic":
                best_video["topic"],

            "views":
                best_video["views"],

            "duration":
                best_video["duration"],
        }

    # ========================================================
    # STRATEJÄ°
    # ========================================================

    strategy = {

        "status":
            "OK",

        "total_videos":
            total_videos,

        "average_duration":
            average_duration,

        "average_images":
            average_images,

        "summary":
            (
                f"{total_videos} video analiz edildi. "
                f"Ortalama video sÃ¼resi "
                f"{average_duration:.2f} saniye. "
                f"Ortalama gÃ¶rsel sayÄ±sÄ± "
                f"{average_images:.2f}."
            ),

        "duration_strategy":
            duration_strategy,

        "visual_strategy":
            image_strategy,

        "image_strategy":
            image_strategy,

        "topic_strategy":
            topic_strategy,

        "winning_topics":
            winning_topics,

        "recommended_topics":
            recommended_topics,

        "hook_strategy":
            hook_strategy,

        "content_strategy":
            content_strategy,

        "tests":
            tests,

        "best_video":
            best_video_strategy,
    }

    return strategy


# ============================================================
# STRATEJÄ°YÄ° JSON OLARAK KAYDET
# ============================================================

def save_strategy(strategy):

    try:

        STRATEGY_FILE.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True
        )

        with open(
            STRATEGY_FILE,
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as file:

            json.dump(
                strategy,
                file,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=4
            )

        return True

    except Exception as error:

        print()
        print(
            f"âš ï¸ Strateji kaydedilemedi: {error}"
        )

        return False

from openai import OpenAI

from config.settings import OPENAI_API_KEY


client = OpenAI(api_key=OPENAI_API_KEY)


def evaluate_content(content):
    response = client.responses.create(
        model="gpt-5.4-mini",
        input=f"""
Sen profesyonel bir YouTube Shorts editÃ¶rÃ¼sÃ¼n.

AÅŸaÄŸÄ±daki iÃ§eriÄŸi deÄŸerlendir.

Ä°Ã‡ERÄ°K:
{content}

Her kriteri 20 Ã¼zerinden puanla:

HOOK
AKICILIK
MERAK
BAÅLIK
SHORTS_UYGUNLUK

Toplam puan 100 Ã¼zerinden olsun.

DeÄŸerlendirmeyi ÅŸu formatta yap:

[PUAN]
Toplam puan: X/100

[DEGERLENDIRME]
HOOK: X/20 â€” kÄ±sa deÄŸerlendirme
AKICILIK: X/20 â€” kÄ±sa deÄŸerlendirme
MERAK: X/20 â€” kÄ±sa deÄŸerlendirme
BAÅLIK: X/20 â€” kÄ±sa deÄŸerlendirme
SHORTS_UYGUNLUK: X/20 â€” kÄ±sa deÄŸerlendirme

[SONUC]
ONAY veya RED

Ã–NEMLÄ°:
Toplam puan 85 veya Ã¼zerindeyse SONUC ONAY olmalÄ±.
Toplam puan 85'in altÄ±ndaysa SONUC RED olmalÄ±.

Sadece bu formatÄ± kullan.
""",
    )

    editor_result = response.output_text

    print()
    print("=== AI EDITÃ–R ===")
    print(editor_result)

    # -----------------------------
    # OTOMATÄ°K KARAR
    # -----------------------------

    decision = "RED"

    for line in editor_result.splitlines():

        if "Toplam puan:" in line:
            try:
                score_text = line.split(":", 1)[1]
                score = int(score_text.split("/")[0].strip())

                if score >= 85:
                    decision = "ONAY"
                else:
                    decision = "RED"

            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                decision = "RED"

    print()
    print("ğŸ¯ OTOMATÄ°K KARAR:", decision)

    return editor_result, decision

STRATEGY_FILE = Path("data") / "current_strategy.json"


# ============================================================
# STRATEJÄ°YÄ° YÃœKLE
# ============================================================

def load_strategy():

    if not STRATEGY_FILE.exists():
        return None

    try:

        with open(
            STRATEGY_FILE,
            "r",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as file:

            return json.load(file)

    except Exception as error:

        print()
        print(
            f"âš ï¸ Strateji okunamadÄ±: {error}"
        )

        return None

import base64
from pathlib import Path

from openai import OpenAI

from config.settings import OPENAI_API_KEY


client = OpenAI(api_key=OPENAI_API_KEY)


def generate_image(prompt, output_path):
    result = client.images.generate(
        model="gpt-image-2",
        prompt=prompt,
        size="1024x1024",
        quality="low",
    )

    image_data = result.data[0].b64_json

    output_path = Path(output_path)
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with open(output_path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(base64.b64decode(image_data))

    return output_path

============================================================
DOSYA: app\content\image_pipeline.py

from pathlib import Path

from app.content.image_generator import generate_image


def generate_images_from_prompt_file(prompt_file, output_folder):
    prompt_file = Path(prompt_file)
    output_folder = Path(output_folder)

    if not prompt_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"GÃ¶rsel prompt dosyasÄ± bulunamadÄ±: {prompt_file}"
        )

    output_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    text = prompt_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    prompts = [
        prompt.strip()
        for prompt in text.split("\n")
        if prompt.strip()
    ]

    if not prompts:
        raise ValueError("GÃ¶rsel promptu bulunamadÄ±.")

    print()
    print(f"ğŸ¨ Toplam {len(prompts)} gÃ¶rsel Ã¼retilecek.")

    generated_files = []

    for index, prompt in enumerate(prompts, start=1):

        print()
        print(f"ğŸ¨ GÃ¶rsel {index}/{len(prompts)} oluÅŸturuluyor...")

        output_path = output_folder / f"{index:02d}.png"

        generate_image(prompt, output_path)

        print(f"âœ… Kaydedildi: {output_path}")

        generated_files.append(output_path)

    print()
    print("ğŸ‰ TÃœM GÃ–RSELLER TAMAMLANDI!")

    return generated_files

from pathlib import Path
import json
import re
import subprocess

from openai import OpenAI

from config.settings import OPENAI_API_KEY


client = OpenAI(api_key=OPENAI_API_KEY)


# ============================================================
# AI ALTYAZI PARÃ‡ALARINI OLUÅTUR
# ============================================================

def create_ai_captions(text):

    response = client.responses.create(
        model="gpt-5.4-mini",
        input=f"""
AÅŸaÄŸÄ±daki TÃ¼rkÃ§e konuÅŸma metnini YouTube Shorts altyazÄ±larÄ± iÃ§in
doÄŸal ve okunabilir kÄ±sa parÃ§alara bÃ¶l.

KURALLAR:

- Metni yaklaÅŸÄ±k 14-18 anlamlÄ± altyazÄ± bloÄŸuna bÃ¶l.
- Her blok yaklaÅŸÄ±k 4-9 kelime olabilir.
- Kelime sayÄ±sÄ±ndan Ã§ok anlam bÃ¼tÃ¼nlÃ¼ÄŸÃ¼nÃ¼ Ã¶nemse.
- DoÄŸal konuÅŸma ritmini koru.
- Birbirine baÄŸlÄ± kelimeleri ayÄ±rma.
- "mi?", "mÄ±?", "mu?", "mÃ¼?" gibi soru eklerini asla tek baÅŸÄ±na bÄ±rakma.
- "ve", "ama", "fakat", "ancak", "Ã§Ã¼nkÃ¼", "ile" gibi baÄŸlaÃ§larÄ±
  anlamsÄ±z ÅŸekilde ayÄ±rma.
- Ã‡ok kÄ±sa anlamsÄ±z parÃ§alar oluÅŸturma.
- Ã‡ok uzun parÃ§alar oluÅŸturma.
- Noktalama iÅŸaretlerini koru.
- Metindeki hiÃ§bir kelimeyi deÄŸiÅŸtirme.
- HiÃ§bir kelimeyi silme.
- Yeni kelime ekleme.
- Kelimelerin sÄ±rasÄ±nÄ± deÄŸiÅŸtirme.

Ã–NCELÄ°K:

1. Anlam bÃ¼tÃ¼nlÃ¼ÄŸÃ¼
2. DoÄŸal konuÅŸma ritmi
3. Okunabilirlik
4. YaklaÅŸÄ±k 14-18 blok

SADECE JSON DÃ–NDÃœR.

FORMAT:

{{
    "captions": [
        "parÃ§a 1",
        "parÃ§a 2",
        "parÃ§a 3"
    ]
}}

METÄ°N:

{text}
""",
    )

    raw = response.output_text.strip()

    raw = re.sub(
        r"^```json\s*",
        "",
        raw,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE
    )

    raw = re.sub(
        r"\s*```$",
        "",
        raw
    )

    data = json.loads(raw)

    captions = data.get("captions", [])

    if not captions:
        raise ValueError(
            "AI altyazÄ± parÃ§alarÄ± oluÅŸturamadÄ±."
        )

    return captions


# ============================================================
# SES SÃœRESÄ°
# ============================================================

def get_audio_duration(audio_file):

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            "ffprobe",
            "-v",
            "error",
            "-show_entries",
            "format=duration",
            "-of",
            "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1",
            str(audio_file),
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=True,
    )

    return float(result.stdout.strip())


# ============================================================
# SRT ZAMAN FORMATI
# ============================================================

def format_srt_time(seconds):

    milliseconds = int(round(seconds * 1000))

    hours = milliseconds // 3_600_000
    milliseconds %= 3_600_000

    minutes = milliseconds // 60_000
    milliseconds %= 60_000

    seconds = milliseconds // 1000
    milliseconds %= 1000

    return (
        f"{hours:02d}:"
        f"{minutes:02d}:"
        f"{seconds:02d},"
        f"{milliseconds:03d}"
    )


# ============================================================
# SRT OLUÅTUR
# ============================================================

def create_subtitle(
    transcript_file,
    audio_file,
    output_file,
):

    transcript_file = Path(transcript_file)
    audio_file = Path(audio_file)
    output_file = Path(output_file)

    if not transcript_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Transkript bulunamadÄ±: {transcript_file}"
        )

    if not audio_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Ses dosyasÄ± bulunamadÄ±: {audio_file}"
        )

    text = transcript_file.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    ).strip()

    if not text:
        raise ValueError(
            "Transkript boÅŸ."
        )

    duration = get_audio_duration(
        audio_file
    )

    print()
    print("=== AI ALTYAZI MOTORU ===")
    print(
        f"ğŸ™ï¸ Ses sÃ¼resi: {duration:.2f} saniye"
    )

    print(
        "ğŸ¤– AI doÄŸal altyazÄ± bloklarÄ±nÄ± oluÅŸturuyor..."
    )

    captions = create_ai_captions(
        text
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ“ {len(captions)} altyazÄ± bloÄŸu oluÅŸturuldu."
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # KARAKTER TOPLAMI
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    total_characters = sum(
        len(caption)
        for caption in captions
    )

    if total_characters <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "AltyazÄ± karakter sayÄ±sÄ± geÃ§ersiz."
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # ZAMANLAMA
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    current_time = 0.0

    srt_blocks = []

    for index, caption in enumerate(
        captions,
        start=1
    ):

        ratio = (
            len(caption)
            / total_characters
        )

        segment_duration = (
            duration * ratio
        )

        start_time = current_time

        if index == len(captions):

            end_time = duration

        else:

            end_time = (
                current_time
                + segment_duration
            )

        srt_blocks.append(
            f"{index}\n"
            f"{format_srt_time(start_time)} --> "
            f"{format_srt_time(end_time)}\n"
            f"{caption}\n"
        )

        current_time = end_time

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # KAYDET
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    output_file.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True
    )

    output_file.write_text(
        "\n".join(srt_blocks),
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    print()
    print("=== ALTYAZI BLOKLARI ===")

    for index, caption in enumerate(
        captions,
        start=1
    ):
        print(
            f"{index:02d}: {caption}"
        )

    print()
    print("âœ… AI ALTYAZI OLUÅTURULDU")
    print(f"Dosya: {output_file}")

    return output_file

from pathlib import Path

from openai import OpenAI

from config.settings import OPENAI_API_KEY


client = OpenAI(api_key=OPENAI_API_KEY)


def transcribe_audio(audio_path):
    audio_path = Path(audio_path)

    if not audio_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Ses dosyasÄ± bulunamadÄ±: {audio_path}"
        )

    with open(audio_path, "rb") as audio_file:
        result = client.audio.transcriptions.create(
            model="gpt-4o-mini-transcribe",
            file=audio_file,
            response_format="json",
        )

    return result

============================================================
DOSYA: app\content\video_creator.py

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess


# ============================================================
# SES SÃœRESÄ°
# ============================================================

def get_audio_duration(audio_file):

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            "ffprobe",
            "-v",
            "error",
            "-show_entries",
            "format=duration",
            "-of",
            "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1",
            str(audio_file),
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=True,
    )

    return float(result.stdout.strip())


# ============================================================
# GÃ–RSELLER + SES â†’ VÄ°DEO
# ============================================================

def create_video(
    image_folder,
    audio_file,
    output_file,
):

    image_folder = Path(image_folder)
    audio_file = Path(audio_file)
    output_file = Path(output_file)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # DOSYA KONTROLLERÄ°
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not image_folder.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"GÃ¶rsel klasÃ¶rÃ¼ bulunamadÄ±: {image_folder}"
        )

    if not audio_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Ses dosyasÄ± bulunamadÄ±: {audio_file}"
        )

    images = sorted(
        image_folder.glob("*.png")
    )

    if not images:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"GÃ¶rsel bulunamadÄ±: {image_folder}"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SES SÃœRESÄ°NÄ° Ã–LÃ‡
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    audio_duration = get_audio_duration(
        audio_file
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # GÃ–RSELLERÄ°N SÃœRESÄ°NÄ° HESAPLA
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    image_duration = (
        audio_duration / len(images)
    )

    print()
    print("=== VÄ°DEO OLUÅTURMA ===")
    print(
        f"ğŸ¨ GÃ¶rsel sayÄ±sÄ±: {len(images)}"
    )
    print(
        f"ğŸ™ï¸ Ses sÃ¼resi: {audio_duration:.2f} saniye"
    )
    print(
        f"â±ï¸ GÃ¶rsel baÅŸÄ±na sÃ¼re: "
        f"{image_duration:.2f} saniye"
    )
    print(
        "ğŸ¬ Video oluÅŸturuluyor..."
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # GEÃ‡Ä°CÄ° GÃ–RSEL LÄ°STESÄ°
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    concat_file = (
        image_folder.parent
        / "image_list.txt"
    )

    with open(
        concat_file,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as file:

        for image in images:

            file.write(
                f"file '{image.resolve()}'\n"
            )

            file.write(
                f"duration {image_duration:.6f}\n"
            )

        # FFmpeg concat demuxer'da son gÃ¶rselin
        # duration deÄŸerinin uygulanmasÄ± iÃ§in
        # son dosyayÄ± tekrar ekliyoruz.

        file.write(
            f"file '{images[-1].resolve()}'\n"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # FFMPEG
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    command = [
        "ffmpeg",
        "-y",

        "-f",
        "concat",

        "-safe",
        "0",

        "-i",
        str(concat_file),

        "-i",
        str(audio_file),

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # SHORTS FORMAT
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        "-vf",
        (
            "scale=1080:1920:"
            "force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
            "crop=1080:1920"
        ),

        "-r",
        "25",

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # VÄ°DEO
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        "-c:v",
        "libx264",

        "-preset",
        "medium",

        "-crf",
        "20",

        "-pix_fmt",
        "yuv420p",

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # SES
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        "-c:a",
        "aac",

        "-b:a",
        "128k",

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # SESÄ° KESME
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        "-t",
        f"{audio_duration:.3f}",

        str(output_file),
    ]

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Ã‡ALIÅTIR
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    subprocess.run(
        command,
        check=True
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # GEÃ‡Ä°CÄ° DOSYAYI SÄ°L
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if concat_file.exists():
        concat_file.unlink()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SONUÃ‡
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not output_file.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "FFmpeg Ã§alÄ±ÅŸtÄ± ancak video oluÅŸmadÄ±."
        )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… VIDEO OLUÅTURULDU")
    print("========================================")
    print()
    print(
        f"Dosya: {output_file}"
    )

    return output_file

DOSYA: app\content\video_pipeline.py

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEOYA ALTYAZI GÃ–MME
# ============================================================

def add_subtitles(
    video_file,
    subtitle_file,
    output_file,
):

    video_file = Path(video_file)
    subtitle_file = Path(subtitle_file)
    output_file = Path(output_file)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # DOSYA KONTROLLERÄ°
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not video_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Video bulunamadÄ±: {video_file}"
        )

    if not subtitle_file.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"AltyazÄ± bulunamadÄ±: {subtitle_file}"
        )

    output_file.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True
    )

    print()
    print("=== VÄ°DEO MOTORU ===")
    print("ğŸ¬ Video hazÄ±rlanÄ±yor...")
    print("ğŸ“ AltyazÄ± videoya gÃ¶mÃ¼lÃ¼yor...")

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # WINDOWS YOLU
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    subtitle_path = str(
        subtitle_file.resolve()
    ).replace("\\", "/")

    # FFmpeg filter iÃ§in Windows sÃ¼rÃ¼cÃ¼sÃ¼ndeki : karakterini kaÃ§Ä±r
    subtitle_path = subtitle_path.replace(
        ":",
        "\\:"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # ALTYAZI STÄ°LÄ°
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    subtitle_style = (
        "FontName=Arial,"
        "FontSize=10,"
        "Bold=1,"
        "PrimaryColour=&H00FFFFFF,"
        "OutlineColour=&H00000000,"
        "BorderStyle=1,"
        "Outline=2,"
        "Shadow=1,"
        "Alignment=8,"
        "MarginV=220"
    )

    subtitle_filter = (
        f"subtitles='{subtitle_path}'"
        f":force_style='{subtitle_style}'"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # FFMPEG
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    command = [
        "ffmpeg",
        "-y",

        "-i",
        str(video_file),

        "-vf",
        subtitle_filter,

        "-c:v",
        "libx264",

        "-preset",
        "medium",

        "-crf",
        "20",

        "-c:a",
        "copy",

        str(output_file),
    ]

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Ã‡ALIÅTIR
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    subprocess.run(
        command,
        check=True
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SONUÃ‡
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not output_file.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "FFmpeg Ã§alÄ±ÅŸtÄ± ancak Ã§Ä±ktÄ± videosu oluÅŸmadÄ±."
        )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… FÄ°NAL VÄ°DEO OLUÅTURULDU")
    print("========================================")
    print()
    print(f"Dosya: {output_file}")

    return output_file

from app.content.content_engine import create_short


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” Ã‡OKLU SHORTS ÃœRETÄ°MÄ°
# ============================================================

def main():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ¬ CREATOR ENGINE â€” Ã‡OKLU ÃœRETÄ°M")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    try:

        count = int(
            input(
                "KaÃ§ video Ã¼retilecek: "
            ).strip()
        )

    except ValueError:

        print()
        print("âŒ GeÃ§erli bir sayÄ± girilmedi.")
        return

    if count <= 0:

        print()
        print("âŒ Video sayÄ±sÄ± 0'dan bÃ¼yÃ¼k olmalÄ±.")
        return

    print()
    print(
        f"ğŸ“¦ {count} video iÃ§in konu girilecek."
    )
    print()

    topics = []

    for number in range(1, count + 1):

        while True:

            topic = input(
                f"{number}. Konu: "
            ).strip()

            if topic:

                topics.append(topic)
                break

            print(
                "âš ï¸ Konu boÅŸ bÄ±rakÄ±lamaz."
            )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸš€ Ã‡OKLU ÃœRETÄ°M BAÅLIYOR")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    success_count = 0
    failed_count = 0

    for index, topic in enumerate(
        topics,
        start=1
    ):

        print()
        print("========================================")
        print(
            f"ğŸ¬ VÄ°DEO {index}/{count}"
        )
        print(
            f"ğŸ“Œ KONU: {topic}"
        )
        print("========================================")
        print()

        try:

            result = create_short(
                topic
            )

            if result is False:

                failed_count += 1

                print()
                print(
                    "âŒ Bu video oluÅŸturulamadÄ±."
                )

            else:

                success_count += 1

                print()
                print(
                    "âœ… Bu video tamamlandÄ±."
                )

        except Exception as error:

            failed_count += 1

            print()
            print(
                "âŒ VÄ°DEO ÃœRETÄ°M HATASI"
            )

            print(
                f"Hata: {error}"
            )

            print()
            print(
                "â¡ï¸ Sonraki videoya geÃ§iliyor..."
            )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“Š Ã‡OKLU ÃœRETÄ°M TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    print(
        f"âœ… BaÅŸarÄ±lÄ±: {success_count}"
    )

    print(
        f"âŒ BaÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z: {failed_count}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ“¦ Toplam: {count}"
    )

    print()
    print("ğŸ§  Analytics kayÄ±tlarÄ± gÃ¼ncellendi.")
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":

    main()

from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3

from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE ANALYTICS SYNC
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

TOKEN_FILE = BASE_DIR / "data" / "youtube_token.json"
DB_FILE = BASE_DIR / "data" / "creator_engine.db"


# ============================================================
# AYAR
# ============================================================

# Ä°lk Ã§alÄ±ÅŸtÄ±rmada False bÄ±rakÄ±yoruz.
# True yaptÄ±ÄŸÄ±mÄ±zda veriler DB'ye yazÄ±lacak.
WRITE_TO_DATABASE = True


# ============================================================
# YOUTUBE
# ============================================================

def get_youtube():

    credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
        TOKEN_FILE
    )

    return build(
        "youtube",
        "v3",
        credentials=credentials
    )


def get_analytics():

    credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
        TOKEN_FILE
    )

    return build(
        "youtubeAnalytics",
        "v2",
        credentials=credentials
    )


# ============================================================
# KANAL VÄ°DEOLARI
# ============================================================

def get_all_video_ids(youtube):

    response = youtube.channels().list(
        part="contentDetails",
        mine=True
    ).execute()

    channels = response.get("items", [])

    if not channels:
        return []

    uploads_playlist = (
        channels[0]
        ["contentDetails"]
        ["relatedPlaylists"]
        ["uploads"]
    )

    video_ids = []
    page_token = None

    while True:

        response = youtube.playlistItems().list(
            part="contentDetails",
            playlistId=uploads_playlist,
            maxResults=50,
            pageToken=page_token
        ).execute()

        for item in response.get("items", []):

            video_ids.append(
                item["contentDetails"]["videoId"]
            )

        page_token = response.get(
            "nextPageToken"
        )

        if not page_token:
            break

    return video_ids


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEO DETAYLARI
# ============================================================

def get_video_details(youtube, video_ids):

    videos = []

    for start in range(
        0,
        len(video_ids),
        50
    ):

        batch = video_ids[
            start:start + 50
        ]

        response = youtube.videos().list(
            part="snippet,contentDetails,statistics",
            id=",".join(batch)
        ).execute()

        videos.extend(
            response.get("items", [])
        )

    return videos


# ============================================================
# SÃœRE
# ============================================================

def parse_duration(duration):

    import re

    match = re.fullmatch(
        r"PT(?:(\d+)H)?(?:(\d+)M)?(?:(\d+)S)?",
        duration
    )

    if not match:
        return 0

    hours = int(match.group(1) or 0)
    minutes = int(match.group(2) or 0)
    seconds = int(match.group(3) or 0)

    return (
        hours * 3600
        + minutes * 60
        + seconds
    )


# ============================================================
# SHORTS BELÄ°RLE
# ============================================================

def get_shorts(videos):

    shorts = []

    for video in videos:

        duration = parse_duration(
            video["contentDetails"]["duration"]
        )

        # Ã–nceden gÃ¶rdÃ¼ÄŸÃ¼mÃ¼z kanal yapÄ±sÄ±na gÃ¶re
        # 4 uzun video + 1 canlÄ± yayÄ±n Shorts deÄŸildir.
        #
        # 3 dakikaya kadar Shorts olabileceÄŸi iÃ§in
        # sadece sÃ¼reye gÃ¶re 60 saniye sÄ±nÄ±rÄ± koymuyoruz.

        title = video["snippet"].get(
            "title",
            ""
        )

        # CanlÄ± yayÄ±n
        if title.lower().find(
            "kanalÄ± canlÄ± yayÄ±nda"
        ) >= 0:

            continue

        # 3 dakikadan uzun iÃ§erikler
        if duration > 180:

            continue

        shorts.append(video)

    return shorts


# ============================================================
# ANALYTICS
# ============================================================

def get_video_analytics(
    analytics,
    video_id
):

    try:

        response = analytics.reports().query(
            ids="channel==MINE",
            startDate="2020-01-01",
            endDate="2030-01-01",
            metrics=(
                "views,"
                "likes,"
                "comments,"
                "estimatedMinutesWatched,"
                "averageViewDuration,"
                "averageViewPercentage"
            ),
            filters=f"video=={video_id}"
        ).execute()

        rows = response.get(
            "rows",
            []
        )

        if not rows:
            return None

        row = rows[0]

        return {
            "views": int(row[0]),
            "likes": int(row[1]),
            "comments": int(row[2]),
            "estimated_minutes": float(row[3]),
            "average_watch_time": float(row[4]),
            "watch_percentage": float(row[5]),
        }

    except Exception as error:

        print(
            f"âš ï¸ Analytics alÄ±namadÄ±: "
            f"{video_id}"
        )

        print(
            f"   {error}"
        )

        return None


# ============================================================
# DB
# ============================================================

def get_existing_video_numbers():

    db = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)

    rows = db.execute(
        """
        SELECT
            video_number,
            youtube_id
        FROM videos
        WHERE youtube_id IS NOT NULL
        """
    ).fetchall()

    db.close()

    return {
        youtube_id: video_number
        for video_number, youtube_id
        in rows
        if youtube_id
    }


def get_next_video_number():

    db = sqlite3.connect(DB_FILE)

    row = db.execute(
        """
        SELECT COALESCE(
            MAX(video_number),
            0
        )
        FROM videos
        """
    ).fetchone()

    db.close()

    return int(row[0]) + 1


# ============================================================
# AKTARIM
# ============================================================

def sync():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ§  CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE ANALYTICS")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    youtube = get_youtube()
    analytics = get_analytics()

    video_ids = get_all_video_ids(
        youtube
    )

    videos = get_video_details(
        youtube,
        video_ids
    )

    shorts = get_shorts(
        videos
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ¬ YouTube videolarÄ±: "
        f"{len(videos)}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ“± Shorts olarak belirlenen: "
        f"{len(shorts)}"
    )

    print()

    existing = get_existing_video_numbers()

    next_number = get_next_video_number()

    db = None

    if WRITE_TO_DATABASE:

        db = sqlite3.connect(
            DB_FILE
        )

    added = 0
    updated = 0
    analytics_ok = 0

    print("----------------------------------------")

    for video in shorts:

        youtube_id = video["id"]

        title = video["snippet"].get(
            "title",
            ""
        )

        published_at = video["snippet"].get(
            "publishedAt",
            ""
        )

        duration = parse_duration(
            video["contentDetails"]["duration"]
        )

        stats = video.get(
            "statistics",
            {}
        )

        views = int(
            stats.get(
                "viewCount",
                0
            )
        )

        likes = int(
            stats.get(
                "likeCount",
                0
            )
        )

        comments = int(
            stats.get(
                "commentCount",
                0
            )
        )

        analytics_data = get_video_analytics(
            analytics,
            youtube_id
        )

        if analytics_data:

            average_watch_time = (
                analytics_data[
                    "average_watch_time"
                ]
            )

            watch_percentage = (
                analytics_data[
                    "watch_percentage"
                ]
            )

            analytics_ok += 1

        else:

            average_watch_time = 0
            watch_percentage = 0

        if youtube_id in existing:

            video_number = existing[
                youtube_id
            ]

            action = "GÃœNCELLE"

            if WRITE_TO_DATABASE:

                db.execute(
                    """
                    UPDATE videos
                    SET
                        topic = ?,
                        duration = ?,
                        published_at = ?,
                        views = ?,
                        likes = ?,
                        comments = ?,
                        average_watch_time = ?,
                        watch_percentage = ?
                    WHERE youtube_id = ?
                    """,
                    (
                        title,
                        duration,
                        published_at,
                        views,
                        likes,
                        comments,
                        average_watch_time,
                        watch_percentage,
                        youtube_id,
                    )
                )

            updated += 1

        else:

            video_number = next_number
            next_number += 1

            action = "YENÄ°"

            if WRITE_TO_DATABASE:

                db.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO videos (
                        video_number,
                        topic,
                        category,
                        hook,
                        duration,
                        image_count,
                        created_at,
                        published_at,
                        views,
                        likes,
                        comments,
                        shares,
                        average_watch_time,
                        watch_percentage,
                        youtube_id
                    )
                    VALUES (
                        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
                    )
                    """,
                    (
                        video_number,
                        title,
                        "youtube",
                        "",
                        duration,
                        0,
                        published_at,
                        published_at,
                        views,
                        likes,
                        comments,
                        0,
                        average_watch_time,
                        watch_percentage,
                        youtube_id,
                    )
                )

            added += 1

        print(
            f"{action:8} "
            f"#{video_number:03d} | "
            f"{duration:3d} sn | "
            f"{views:7d} izlenme | "
            f"{title}"
        )

    if db:

        db.commit()
        db.close()

    print("----------------------------------------")

    print()
    print(
        f"ğŸ†• Yeni: {added}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ”„ GÃ¼ncellenen: {updated}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ“Š Analytics alÄ±nan: "
        f"{analytics_ok}/{len(shorts)}"
    )

    if WRITE_TO_DATABASE:

        print()
        print(
            "ğŸ’¾ Veriler veritabanÄ±na yazÄ±ldÄ±."
        )

    else:

        print()
        print(
            "âš ï¸ Ã–NÄ°ZLEME MODU"
        )

        print(
            "ğŸ’¾ VeritabanÄ±na hiÃ§bir deÄŸiÅŸiklik yapÄ±lmadÄ±."
        )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… SENKRONÄ°ZASYON TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()


# ============================================================
# Ã‡ALIÅTIR
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    sync()
