from pathlib import Path

from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE ANALYTICS TEST
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

TOKEN_FILE = (
    BASE_DIR
    / "data"
    / "youtube_token.json"
)


# ============================================================
# YOUTUBE ANALYTICS BAÄLANTISI
# ============================================================

def get_analytics():

    if not TOKEN_FILE.exists():

        print()
        print("âŒ YouTube token bulunamadÄ±.")
        print(
            f"ğŸ“ {TOKEN_FILE}"
        )

        return None

    credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
        TOKEN_FILE
    )

    analytics = build(
        "youtubeAnalytics",
        "v2",
        credentials=credentials
    )

    return analytics


# ============================================================
# ANALYTICS TEST
# ============================================================

def test_analytics():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“Š CREATOR ENGINE â€” ANALYTICS TEST")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    analytics = get_analytics()

    if analytics is None:

        return

    print("ğŸ”Œ YouTube Analytics API baÄŸlantÄ±sÄ± baÅŸarÄ±lÄ±.")
    print()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Son 30 gÃ¼n iÃ§in kanal Ã¶zeti
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    response = analytics.reports().query(
        ids="channel==MINE",
        startDate="2026-07-29",
        endDate="2026-08-28",
        metrics=(
            "views,"
            "likes,"
            "comments,"
            "estimatedMinutesWatched,"
            "averageViewDuration,"
            "averageViewPercentage"
        )
    ).execute()

    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“ˆ ANALYTICS VERÄ°LERÄ°")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    column_headers = response.get(
        "columnHeaders",
        []
    )

    rows = response.get(
        "rows",
        []
    )

    if not rows:

        print("âš ï¸ SeÃ§ilen tarih aralÄ±ÄŸÄ±nda veri bulunamadÄ±.")

    else:

        headers = [
            column["name"]
            for column in column_headers
        ]

        print(
            " | ".join(headers)
        )

        print("----------------------------------------")

        for row in rows:

            print(
                " | ".join(
                    str(value)
                    for value in row
                )
            )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… ANALYTICS TESTÄ° TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()


# ============================================================
# TEST
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    test_analytics()

from pathlib import Path

from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE OAUTH
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

# Google Cloud'dan indirdiÄŸin OAuth JSON dosyasÄ±
CLIENT_SECRET_FILE = (
    BASE_DIR
    / "client_secret_150956391796-vpb251vj5d9dib3qccgpjqjmmnjmv4p9.apps.googleusercontent.com.json"
)

# OAuth sonrasÄ± oluÅŸturulacak token
TOKEN_FILE = (
    BASE_DIR
    / "data"
    / "youtube_token.json"
)


# ============================================================
# YOUTUBE Ä°ZÄ°NLERÄ°
# ============================================================

SCOPES = [
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.readonly",
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/yt-analytics.readonly",
]


# ============================================================
# OAUTH BAÄLANTISI
# ============================================================

def authenticate_youtube():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ” CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE BAÄLANTISI")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    if not CLIENT_SECRET_FILE.exists():

        print("âŒ OAuth JSON dosyasÄ± bulunamadÄ±.")
        print()
        print(
            f"Aranan dosya:"
        )
        print(
            CLIENT_SECRET_FILE
        )

        return None

    credentials = None

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Daha Ã¶nce alÄ±nmÄ±ÅŸ token varsa kullan
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if TOKEN_FILE.exists():

        try:

            credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
                TOKEN_FILE,
                SCOPES,
            )

            print("âœ… Mevcut YouTube yetkilendirmesi bulundu.")

        except Exception as error:

            print()
            print(
                f"âš ï¸ Mevcut token okunamadÄ±: {error}"
            )

            credentials = None

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Yeni OAuth baÄŸlantÄ±sÄ±
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if credentials is None or not credentials.valid:

        print()
        print("ğŸŒ Google OAuth baÅŸlatÄ±lÄ±yor...")
        print()
        print(
            "TarayÄ±cÄ± aÃ§Ä±lacak."
        )
        print(
            "33 Shorts bulunan YouTube kanalÄ±nÄ±n"
        )
        print(
            "Google hesabÄ±yla giriÅŸ yap."
        )
        print()

        try:

            flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file(
                CLIENT_SECRET_FILE,
                SCOPES,
            )

            credentials = flow.run_local_server(
                port=0
            )

        except Exception as error:

            print()
            print(
                f"âŒ OAuth baÄŸlantÄ±sÄ± baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z: {error}"
            )

            return None

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # Token kaydet
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        TOKEN_FILE.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True
        )

        TOKEN_FILE.write_text(
            credentials.to_json(),
            encoding="utf-8"
        )

        print()
        print("âœ… YouTube yetkilendirmesi tamamlandÄ±.")
        print()
        print(
            f"ğŸ” Token kaydedildi:"
        )
        print(
            TOKEN_FILE
        )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… YOUTUBE BAÄLANTISI HAZIR")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    return credentials


# ============================================================
# TEST
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    authenticate_youtube()

from pathlib import Path

from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE VIDEO LÄ°STELEME
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

TOKEN_FILE = (
    BASE_DIR
    / "data"
    / "youtube_token.json"
)


# ============================================================
# YOUTUBE BAÄLANTISI
# ============================================================

def get_youtube():

    if not TOKEN_FILE.exists():

        print()
        print("âŒ YouTube token bulunamadÄ±.")
        print(
            f"ğŸ“ {TOKEN_FILE}"
        )

        return None

    credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
        TOKEN_FILE
    )

    youtube = build(
        "youtube",
        "v3",
        credentials=credentials
    )

    return youtube


# ============================================================
# KANAL VÄ°DEOLARINI BUL
# ============================================================

def get_all_videos(youtube):

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Ã–nce kanalÄ±n uploads playlist ID'sini al
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    channel_response = youtube.channels().list(
        part="contentDetails",
        mine=True
    ).execute()

    channels = channel_response.get(
        "items",
        []
    )

    if not channels:

        return []

    uploads_playlist_id = (
        channels[0]
        ["contentDetails"]
        ["relatedPlaylists"]
        ["uploads"]
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Uploads playlistinden videolarÄ± al
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    videos = []

    next_page_token = None

    while True:

        response = youtube.playlistItems().list(
            part="snippet,contentDetails",
            playlistId=uploads_playlist_id,
            maxResults=50,
            pageToken=next_page_token
        ).execute()

        videos.extend(
            response.get(
                "items",
                []
            )
        )

        next_page_token = response.get(
            "nextPageToken"
        )

        if not next_page_token:

            break

    return videos


# ============================================================
# VÄ°DEO LÄ°STESÄ°NÄ° YAZDIR
# ============================================================

def list_videos():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“º CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE VÄ°DEOLARI")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    youtube = get_youtube()

    if youtube is None:

        return

    videos = get_all_videos(
        youtube
    )

    if not videos:

        print("âŒ Video bulunamadÄ±.")
        return

    print(
        f"ğŸ¬ Toplam bulunan video: "
        f"{len(videos)}"
    )

    print()

    print("----------------------------------------")

    for index, video in enumerate(
        videos,
        start=1
    ):

        snippet = video["snippet"]

        video_id = (
            video["contentDetails"]
            ["videoId"]
        )

        title = snippet.get(
            "title",
            ""
        )

        published_at = snippet.get(
            "publishedAt",
            ""
        )

        print(
            f"#{index:02d} | "
            f"{video_id} | "
            f"{published_at} | "
            f"{title}"
        )

    print("----------------------------------------")

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… VÄ°DEO LÄ°STESÄ° TAMAMLANDI")
    print("========================================")
    print()


# ============================================================
# TEST
# ============================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    list_videos()

from pathlib import Path

from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials


# ============================================================
# CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE BAÄLANTI TESTÄ°
# ============================================================

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

TOKEN_FILE = (
    BASE_DIR
    / "data"
    / "youtube_token.json"
)


# ============================================================
# YOUTUBE BAÄLANTISI
# ============================================================

def get_youtube():

    if not TOKEN_FILE.exists():

        print()
        print("âŒ YouTube token bulunamadÄ±.")
        print(
            f"ğŸ“ {TOKEN_FILE}"
        )

        return None

    credentials = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(
        TOKEN_FILE
    )

    youtube = build(
        "youtube",
        "v3",
        credentials=credentials
    )

    return youtube


# ============================================================
# KANAL BÄ°LGÄ°LERÄ°
# ============================================================

def test_channel():

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“º CREATOR ENGINE â€” YOUTUBE TEST")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    youtube = get_youtube()

    if youtube is None:

        return

    print("ğŸ”Œ YouTube API baÄŸlantÄ±sÄ± baÅŸarÄ±lÄ±.")
    print()

    response = youtube.channels().list(
        part="snippet,statistics",
        mine=True
    ).execute()

    channels = response.get(
        "items",
        []
    )

    if not channels:

        print("âŒ Kanal bulunamadÄ±.")
        return

    channel = channels[0]

    snippet = channel["snippet"]
    statistics = channel["statistics"]

    print("========================================")
    print("ğŸ“º KANAL BÄ°LGÄ°LERÄ°")
    print("========================================")
    print()

    print(
        f"ğŸ“› Kanal adÄ±: "
        f"{snippet.get('title', '')}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ†” Kanal ID: "
        f"{channel.get('id', '')}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ‘¥ Abone: "
        f"{statistics.get('subscriberCount', 'Gizli')}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ¬ Toplam video: "
        f"{statistics.get('videoCount', 0)}"
    )

    print(
        f"ğŸ‘ï¸ Toplam gÃ¶rÃ¼ntÃ¼lenme: "
        f"{statistics.get('viewCount', 0)}"
    )

    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("âœ… YOUTUBE BAÄLANTI TESTÄ° BAÅARILI")
    print("========================================")
    print()

# TEST

if __name__ == "__main__":

    test_channel()
